Background desk (blotches + fibres + crease + vignette):
*`reference-v3/src/Bg.tsx`*
```tsx
import React from 'react';
import {AbsoluteFill, random, useCurrentFrame} from 'remotion';
import {PAL} from './theme';
import {Layer} from './paper';

/** warm paper desk: blotches, fibres, creases, vignette. (grain + rough edges are global filters) */
export const Bg: React.FC = () => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame();
  return (
    <AbsoluteFill style={{background: PAL.cream}}>
      <Layer depth={0.12}>
        <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: -200, top: -200, width: 1400, height: 1000, background: 'radial-gradient(ellipse, rgba(255,250,235,0.9), transparent 65%)'}} />
        <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 900, top: 400, width: 1300, height: 1000, background: 'radial-gradient(ellipse, rgba(205,170,115,0.32), transparent 65%)'}} />
        <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: -100, top: 600, width: 900, height: 700, background: 'radial-gradient(ellipse, rgba(214,185,133,0.28), transparent 65%)'}} />
        {Array.from({length: 90}).map((_, i) => {
          const x = random(`fx${i}`) * 2000 - 40, y = random(`fy${i}`) * 1150 - 40, l = 8 + random(`fl${i}`) * 26, a = random(`fa${i}`) * 180;
          return <div key={i} style={{position: 'absolute', left: x, top: y, width: l, height: 1.6, background: i % 2 ? 'rgba(120,85,40,0.22)' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.55)', transform: `rotate(${a}deg)`}} />;
        })}
      </Layer>
      <AbsoluteFill style={{background: 'linear-gradient(90deg, transparent 49.6%, rgba(90,60,30,0.07) 50%, rgba(255,255,255,0.28) 50.3%, transparent 51%)'}} />
      <AbsoluteFill style={{background: 'radial-gradient(ellipse at center, transparent 52%, rgba(105,70,30,0.30) 100%)'}} />
    </AbsoluteFill>
  );
};
```

## 5.7 Shape-making code (real)
`roughPoly` subdivides each edge every ~20 px and displaces the points along the edge normal by a seeded random amount (+-`amp` px, 8 % of points get a 2.4x "bite"), producing scissor/torn outlines. Seeds come from Remotion's deterministic `random(seed)`, so every frame renders the same shape (no flicker) unless you change the seed on purpose.
*`reference-v3/src/paper.tsx (roughPoly)`*
```tsx
export const roughPoly = (pts: Pt[], amp: number, seed: string, step = 20): string => {
  const out: Pt[] = [];
  pts.forEach((p, i) => {
    const q = pts[(i + 1) % pts.length];
    const len = Math.hypot(q[0] - p[0], q[1] - p[1]) || 1;
    const n = Math.max(1, Math.round(len / step));
    const nx = -(q[1] - p[1]) / len, ny = (q[0] - p[0]) / len;
    for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
      const t = k / n;
      const bite = rnd(`${seed}b${i}_${k}`) > 0.92 ? 2.4 : 1;
      const j = (rnd(`${seed}e${i}_${k}`) - 0.5) * 2 * amp * bite;
      out.push([p[0] + (q[0] - p[0]) * t + nx * j, p[1] + (q[1] - p[1]) * t + ny * j]);
    }
  });
  return 'M' + out.map((p) => p[0].toFixed(1) + ',' + p[1].toFixed(1)).join('L') + 'Z';
};
```
*`reference-v3/src/paper.tsx (Cut)`*
```tsx
export const Cut: React.FC<{pts: Pt[]; color: string; seed: string; amp?: number; fiber?: number; tex?: boolean; pattern?: 'dots' | 'stripes' | 'grid'; patColor?: string; step?: number; rim?: string}> = ({pts, color, seed, amp = 3, fiber = 5, tex = true, pattern, patColor = 'rgba(30,20,10,0.9)', step = 22, rim = PAL.fiber}) => {
  const id = useId().replace(/:/g, '');
  const {mainD, fibD, box} = useMemo(() => {
    const xs = pts.map((p) => p[0]), ys = pts.map((p) => p[1]);
    const m = fiber + amp * 2.6 + 6;
    return {mainD: roughPoly(pts, amp, seed, step), fibD: fiber > 0 ? roughPoly(expand(pts, fiber), amp * 1.3, seed + 'f', step) : '', box: [Math.min(...xs) - m, Math.min(...ys) - m, Math.max(...xs) - Math.min(...xs) + 2 * m, Math.max(...ys) - Math.min(...ys) + 2 * m]};
  }, [JSON.stringify(pts), seed, amp, fiber, step]);
  return (
    <svg style={{position: 'absolute', left: box[0], top: box[1], overflow: 'visible', pointerEvents: 'none'}} width={box[2]} height={box[3]} viewBox={`${box[0]} ${box[1]} ${box[2]} ${box[3]}`}>
      {pattern && (
        <defs>
          <pattern id={`${id}p`} patternUnits="userSpaceOnUse" width={18} height={18} patternTransform={pattern === 'stripes' ? 'rotate(45)' : undefined}>
            {pattern === 'dots' && <circle cx={9} cy={9} r={3.4} fill={patColor} opacity={0.2} />}
            {pattern === 'stripes' && <rect x={0} y={0} width={7} height={18} fill={patColor} opacity={0.16} />}
            {pattern === 'grid' && <path d="M0 0H18M0 0V18" stroke={patColor} strokeWidth={1.4} opacity={0.18} fill="none" />}
          </pattern>
        </defs>
      )}
      {fibD && <path d={fibD} fill={rim} />}
      <path d={mainD} fill={color} filter={tex ? 'url(#paperTex)' : undefined} />
      {pattern && <path d={mainD} fill={`url(#${id}p)`} />}
    </svg>
  );
};
```
`Cut` draws two paths: a slightly expanded cream "fibre" rim (the torn edge) and the coloured paper on top with `paperTex`, plus optional `pattern` overlays (dots r=3.4 at 20 % alpha on an 18 px grid; stripes 7/18 px at 16 %; grid lines). Typical parameters: `amp` 2.4-3.4 for text strips/tiles, 6-9 for large shapes; `fiber` 4-6 (large 7-9); `step` 22 (default) or 46 for the huge wipe sheets.
*`reference-v3/src/paper.tsx (Piece)`*
```tsx
export const Piece: React.FC<{x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; rot?: number; color: string; seed: string; amp?: number; fiber?: number; tex?: boolean;
  pattern?: 'dots' | 'stripes' | 'grid'; patColor?: string; shadow?: boolean; tf?: string; origin?: string; inner?: React.CSSProperties; style?: React.CSSProperties; children?: React.ReactNode; step?: number}> =
  ({x, y, w, h, rot = 0, color, seed, amp = 3, fiber = 5, tex = true, pattern, patColor, shadow = true, tf = '', origin = '50% 50%', inner, style, children, step}) => (
  <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: x, top: y, width: w, height: h, transform: `rotate(${rot}deg) ${tf}`, transformOrigin: origin, filter: shadow ? SHADOW : undefined, ...style}}>
    <Cut pts={rectPts(w, h)} color={color} seed={seed} amp={amp} fiber={fiber} tex={tex} pattern={pattern} patColor={patColor} step={step} />
    <div style={{position: 'absolute', inset: 0, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'center', ...inner}}>{children}</div>
  </div>
);
```
Arrival, jitter and hand-drawn strokes:
*`reference-v3/src/paper.tsx (Land)`*
```tsx
export const Land: React.FC<{at: number; dur?: number; x?: number; y?: number; rot?: number; s?: number; over?: number; children: React.ReactNode; style?: React.CSSProperties; origin?: string}> = ({at, dur = 9, x = 0, y = 0, rot = 0, s = 0, over = 0.07, children, style, origin = '50% 50%'}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame();
  const p = lp(f, at, dur, over);
  return (
    <div style={{opacity: p > 0.001 ? 1 : 0, transform: `translate(${x * (1 - p)}px, ${y * (1 - p)}px) rotate(${rot * (1 - p)}deg) scale(${1 + s * (1 - p)})`, transformOrigin: origin, ...style}}>{children}</div>
  );
};
```
*`reference-v3/src/paper.tsx (useBoil)`*
```tsx
export const useBoil = (key: string, amp = 1.6, rot = 0.6, every = 3) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame();
  const s = Math.floor(f / every);
  return `translate(${(rnd(key + s + 'x') - 0.5) * amp * 2}px, ${(rnd(key + s + 'y') - 0.5) * amp * 2}px) rotate(${(rnd(key + s + 'r') - 0.5) * rot * 2}deg)`;
};
```
*`reference-v3/src/paper.tsx (smooth)`*
```tsx
export const smooth = (p: Pt[]): string => {
  if (p.length < 3) return 'M' + p.map((q) => q.join(',')).join('L');
  let d = `M${p[0][0]},${p[0][1]}`;
  for (let i = 0; i < p.length - 1; i++) {
    const p0 = p[i - 1] ?? p[i], p1 = p[i], p2 = p[i + 1], p3 = p[i + 2] ?? p2;
    d += `C${p1[0] + (p2[0] - p0[0]) / 6},${p1[1] + (p2[1] - p0[1]) / 6} ${p2[0] - (p3[0] - p1[0]) / 6},${p2[1] - (p3[1] - p1[1]) / 6} ${p2[0]},${p2[1]}`;
  }
  return d;
};
```
*`reference-v3/src/paper.tsx (loopD)`*
```tsx
export const loopD = (cx: number, cy: number, rx: number, ry: number, seed: string, turns = 1.15) => {
  const n = 16; const pts: Pt[] = Array.from({length: n + 1}, (_, i) => { const a = -2.2 + (i / n) * Math.PI * 2 * turns; const k = 1 + (i / n) * 0.06 + (rnd(seed + i) - 0.5) * 0.06; return [cx + Math.cos(a) * rx * k, cy + Math.sin(a) * ry * k] as Pt; });
  return smooth(pts);
};
```
*`reference-v3/src/paper.tsx (arrowD)`*
```tsx
export const arrowD = (a: Pt, b: Pt, bend: number, seed: string) => {
  const mx = (a[0] + b[0]) / 2 - (b[1] - a[1]) * bend, my = (a[1] + b[1]) / 2 + (b[0] - a[0]) * bend;
  const ang = Math.atan2(b[1] - my, b[0] - mx);
  const head = (s: number) => `M${b[0]},${b[1]}L${b[0] - Math.cos(ang + s) * 26},${b[1] - Math.sin(ang + s) * 26}`;
  return {shaft: smooth(wob([a, [mx, my], b], 4, seed)), head: head(0.5) + head(-0.5)};
};
```
*`reference-v3/src/paper.tsx (Stroke)`*
```tsx
export const Stroke: React.FC<{d: string; at: number; dur: number; color?: string; width?: number; opacity?: number; style?: React.CSSProperties}> = ({d, at, dur, color = PAL.ink, width = 7, opacity = 1, style}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame();
  const p = clamp01((f - at) / dur);
  if (p <= 0) return null;
  const e = 1 - Math.pow(1 - p, 2);
  return (
    <svg width={1} height={1} style={{position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: 0, overflow: 'visible', pointerEvents: 'none', ...style}}>
      <path d={d} fill="none" stroke={color} strokeWidth={width} strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" pathLength={1} strokeDasharray={1} strokeDashoffset={1 - e} opacity={opacity} />
    </svg>
  );
};
```
*`reference-v3/src/paper.tsx (Marker)`*

```tsx
export const Marker: React.FC<{x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; at: number; dur?: number; color?: string; rot?: number; seed?: string}> = ({x, y, w, h, at, dur = 12, color = PAL.mustard, rot = -1, seed = 'mk'}) => {

  if (p <= 0) return null;
  return (
    <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: x, top: y, width: w, height: h, transform: `rotate(${rot}deg)`, clipPath: `inset(-20px ${(1 - (1 - Math.pow(1 - p, 2))) * 100}% -20px -20px)`}}>
      <Cut pts={rectPts(w, h)} color={color} seed={seed} amp={4} fiber={0} tex={false} step={16} />
    </div>
  );
};
```
*`reference-v3/src/paper.tsx (Tape)`*
```tsx
export const Tape: React.FC<{x: number; y: number; w?: number; h?: number; rot?: number; color?: string; at?: number}> = ({x, y, w = 150, h = 44, rot = -6, color = 'rgba(232,206,140,0.82)', at}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame();
  const p = at === undefined ? 1 : lp(f, at, 5, 0.1);
  const pts: Pt[] = []; const z = 7;
  for (let i = 0; i <= 5; i++) pts.push([i % 2 ? -z : 0, (h * i) / 5]);
  const right: Pt[] = []; for (let i = 5; i >= 0; i--) right.push([w + (i % 2 ? z : 0), (h * i) / 5]);
  return (
    <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: x, top: y, width: w, height: h, opacity: p > 0.01 ? 0.96 : 0, transform: `rotate(${rot}deg) scale(${0.6 + 0.4 * Math.min(p, 1.1)})`}}>
      <Cut pts={[...pts.map((q) => [q[0], q[1]] as Pt), ...right]} color={color} seed={`tape${x}${y}`} amp={0.8} fiber={0} tex={false} step={60} />
      <div style={{position: 'absolute', inset: 0, backgroundImage: 'repeating-linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(255,255,255,0.18) 0 2px, transparent 2px 7px)'}} />
    </div>
  );
};
```

```tsx
export const Stamp: React.FC<{text: string; at: number; size?: number; color?: string; rot?: number; font?: string; x?: number; y?: number; weight?: number}> = ({text, at, size = 64, color = PAL.tomato, rot = -8, font = SERIF, x = 0, y = 0, weight = 700}) => {

  return (
    <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: x, top: y, opacity: p > 0.01 ? 0.95 : 0, transform: `rotate(${rot}deg) scale(${1 + (1 - Math.min(p, 1)) * 1.1})`, transformOrigin: '50% 50%'}}>
      <div style={{border: `${Math.max(4, size * 0.1)}px solid ${color}`, borderRadius: size * 0.25, padding: `${size * 0.08}px ${size * 0.35}px`, color, fontFamily: font, fontWeight: weight, fontSize: size, whiteSpace: 'nowrap', filter: 'url(#stampWear)', lineHeight: 1.2}}>{text}</div>
    </div>
  );
};
```
*`reference-v3/src/paper.tsx (Layer)`*
```tsx
export const Layer: React.FC<{depth: number; children: React.ReactNode; style?: React.CSSProperties}> = ({depth, children, style}) => {
  const c = useContext(CamCtx);
  return <div style={{position: 'absolute', inset: 0, transform: `translate(${-c.x * depth}px, ${-c.y * depth}px)`, ...style}}>{children}</div>;
};
```
Stroke note: `pathLength={1}` + `strokeDasharray={1}` + `strokeDashoffset={1-e}` draws any path without measuring it. Marker highlights sit BEHIND text and reveal with `clipPath: inset(... right %)`.

## 5.8 Icons: built from the same primitives
Icons are tiny SVGs inside a `Sticker` (cream outline via the `sticker` filter) using flat palette fills, 3-4 px navy/ink outlines (`strokeLinejoin round`) and 1-2 highlights; they are not rough-cut, so the global `rough` filter supplies the wobble. The origami tetrahedron is the one piece of real geometry: 4 vertices of a regular tetrahedron, rotate by `rotY`/`rotX`, project orthographically, depth-sort the 4 faces, shade each face by its normal, and let `fold[i]` (0..1 from a `lp` per face) scale each face in from its centroid so faces "tape down" one by one.
*`reference-v3/src/icons.tsx (lines 1-51)`*
```tsx
import React from 'react';
import {PAL} from './theme';
import {clamp01} from './timeline';

const Sticker: React.FC<{w: number; h: number; size: number; children: React.ReactNode; style?: React.CSSProperties; rim?: boolean}> = ({w, h, size, children, style, rim = true}) => (
  <svg width={size} height={(size * h) / w} viewBox={`0 0 ${w} ${h}`} style={{overflow: 'visible', ...style}}>
    <g filter={rim ? 'url(#sticker)' : undefined}><g filter="url(#paperTex)">{children}</g></g>
  </svg>
);

// ------------------------------------------------ origami tetrahedron (flat shaded folded paper)
const k = 1 / Math.sqrt(3);
const V: [number, number, number][] = [[k, k, k], [k, -k, -k], [-k, k, -k], [-k, -k, k]];
const FACES = [[0, 1, 2], [0, 1, 3], [0, 2, 3], [1, 2, 3]];
const FCOL = [PAL.mustard, PAL.tomato, PAL.teal, PAL.pink];
export const Origami: React.FC<{size: number; rotY: number; rotX?: number; fold?: number[]; colors?: string[]}> = ({size, rotY, rotX = 0.5, fold = [1, 1, 1, 1], colors = FCOL}) => {
  const cy = Math.cos(rotY), sy = Math.sin(rotY), cx = Math.cos(rotX), sx = Math.sin(rotX);
  const P3 = V.map(([x, y, z]) => { const X = x * cy + z * sy; let Z = -x * sy + z * cy; const Y = y * cx - Z * sx; Z = y * sx + Z * cx; return [X, Y, Z]; });
  const P = P3.map(([X, Y, Z]) => { const s = 3.2 / (3.2 - Z); return [X * s * size * 0.5, -Y * s * size * 0.5, Z]; });
  const L = [-0.4, 0.7, 0.6];
  const faces = FACES.map((f, i) => {
    const a = P3[f[0]], b = P3[f[1]], c = P3[f[2]];
    const u = [b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1], b[2] - a[2]], v = [c[0] - a[0], c[1] - a[1], c[2] - a[2]];
    let n = [u[1] * v[2] - u[2] * v[1], u[2] * v[0] - u[0] * v[2], u[0] * v[1] - u[1] * v[0]];
    const m = Math.hypot(n[0], n[1], n[2]); n = n.map((q) => q / m);
    const cen = [(a[0] + b[0] + c[0]) / 3, (a[1] + b[1] + c[1]) / 3, (a[2] + b[2] + c[2]) / 3];
    if (n[0] * cen[0] + n[1] * cen[1] + n[2] * cen[2] < 0) n = n.map((q) => -q);
    const lit = clamp01(0.5 + 0.5 * (n[0] * L[0] + n[1] * L[1] + n[2] * L[2]));
    return {f, i, z: (P[f[0]][2] + P[f[1]][2] + P[f[2]][2]) / 3, lit};
  }).sort((p, q) => p.z - q.z);
  return (
    <svg width={size} height={size} viewBox={`${-size / 2} ${-size / 2} ${size} ${size}`} style={{overflow: 'visible'}}>
      <g filter="url(#paperTex)">
        {faces.map(({f, i, lit}) => {
          const pts = f.map((v) => [P[v][0], P[v][1]]);
          const cxx = (pts[0][0] + pts[1][0] + pts[2][0]) / 3, cyy = (pts[0][1] + pts[1][1] + pts[2][1]) / 3;
          const p = clamp01(fold[i]);
          return (
            <g key={i} transform={`translate(${cxx} ${cyy - (1 - p) * size * 0.5}) rotate(${(1 - p) * (i % 2 ? 40 : -40)}) scale(${0.3 + 0.7 * p}) translate(${-cxx} ${-cyy})`} opacity={p > 0.01 ? 1 : 0}>
              <polygon points={pts.map((q) => q.join(',')).join(' ')} fill={colors[i]} stroke={PAL.fiber} strokeWidth={size * 0.012} strokeLinejoin="round" />
              <polygon points={pts.map((q) => q.join(',')).join(' ')} fill={lit > 0.5 ? '#fff' : '#2a1608'} opacity={Math.abs(lit - 0.5) * 0.7} />
              <polygon points={pts.map((q) => q.join(',')).join(' ')} fill="none" stroke="rgba(255,255,255,0.45)" strokeWidth={size * 0.006} strokeDasharray={`${size * 0.02} ${size * 0.015}`} transform={`translate(${cxx} ${cyy}) scale(0.9) translate(${-cxx} ${-cyy})`} />
            </g>
          );
        })}
      </g>
    </svg>
  );
};

// ------------------------------------------------ friendly paper agent (robot)
```

## 5.9 Per-scene diorama recipes (real code excerpts)
General recipe: 1) 2-3 far shapes in the scene's colour world; 2) one hero object built from 5-15 paper pieces; 3) text on label strips; 4) hand-drawn annotations (underline / circle / arrow) that draw on a later cue; 5) tape or stickers on arrival; 6) everything lands on a cue.

**Title** (`scenes/Title.tsx`, full code in the appendix): cold open strips + origami (sun disc navy, teal triangle behind, pink block, spinning star, paper plane after the drop). The strips move from their cold-open stack to a row via `m` = smoothstep over 16 frames after the drop, while "noobli" tiles drop in. Key lines:
*`reference-v3/src/scenes/Title.tsx (Strip)`*
```tsx
const Strip: React.FC<{i: number; m: number}> = ({i, m}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame();
  const s = STRIPS[i];
  const size = 150;
  const w = tw(s.t, size) + 90, h = 200;
  const wTitle = w * 0.667;
  // cold-open position -> title position
  const cx0 = 110, cy0 = 190 + i * 215;
  const xs = [110, 110 + 690 * 0.667 + 34, 110 + 2 * (690 * 0.667 + 34)];
  const cx1 = xs[i], cy1 = 585;
  const x = cx0 + (cx1 - cx0) * m, y = cy0 + (cy1 - cy0) * m, sc = 1 - 0.333 * m;
  const at = cue(s.at);
  const stampy = i === 2;
  const p = lp(f, at, stampy ? 6 : 10, 0.06);
  const dx = s.from[0] * (1 - p), dy = s.from[1] * (1 - p);
  const stampScale = stampy ? 1 + (1 - Math.min(p, 1)) * 0.7 : 1;
  const boil = useBoil('strip' + i, 1.2, 0.5);
  const circ = loopD(w / 2, h / 2, w / 2 + 70, h / 2 + 45, 'cl', 1.12);
  return (
    <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: x, top: y, width: w, height: h, opacity: p > 0.001 ? 1 : 0, transformOrigin: '0 0', transform: `translate(${dx}px, ${dy}px) scale(${sc * stampScale}) rotate(${s.rot + s.r * (1 - p)}deg) ${boil}`}}>
      <Piece x={0} y={0} w={w} h={h} color={s.c} seed={`strip${i}`} amp={3.4} fiber={6} pattern={i === 1 ? 'stripes' : i === 0 ? 'dots' : undefined} patColor="#000">
        <div style={{fontFamily: SERIF, fontWeight: 700, fontSize: size, color: s.tc, letterSpacing: 4, WebkitTextStroke: `3px ${s.tc}`, lineHeight: 1, whiteSpace: 'nowrap'}}>{s.t}</div>
      </Piece>
      {i === 2 && <Stroke d={circ} at={cue('open.circle')} dur={14} color={PAL.tomato} width={12} />}
    </div>
  );
};
```
*`reference-v3/src/scenes/Title.tsx (Letter)`*
```tsx
const Letter: React.FC<{i: number; drop: number}> = ({i, drop}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame();
  const x0 = 118 + LW.slice(0, i).reduce((a, b) => a + b + 8, 0);
  const p = lp(f, drop + i * 4, 8, 0.1);
  const rot = [-4, 3, -2, 4, -3, 2][i];
  const boil = useBoil('L' + i, 1.5, 0.8);
  return (
    <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: x0, top: 175 + (i % 2) * 14, width: LW[i], height: 260, opacity: p > 0.001 ? 1 : 0, transform: `translateY(${-(1 - p) * 700}px) rotate(${rot + (1 - p) * (i % 2 ? 30 : -30)}deg) ${boil}`}}>
      <Piece x={0} y={0} w={LW[i]} h={260} color={LC[i]} seed={`let${i}`} amp={3.2} fiber={6} pattern={i % 3 === 0 ? 'dots' : undefined} patColor="#000">
        <div style={{fontFamily: DISP, fontSize: 250, color: LT[i], lineHeight: 1, marginTop: -26}}>{LETTERS[i]}</div>
      </Piece>
    </div>
  );
};
```

**Intro**: "我是 / noobli" tiles, strip + mustard highlighter block, robot, three cards (bulb / laptop / notebook) with hand-drawn arrows, tagline strip with marker swipe. (full code in appendix)

**Principles**: counter badge (ticks 01->05) + five stacked cards each with a coloured number block (dots/stripes alternate), title, optional sub-line, hand underline drawn 12 frames after landing, tape piece whose offset varies per card.
*`reference-v3/src/scenes/Principles.tsx (Card)`*
```tsx
const Card: React.FC<{i: number}> = ({i}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame();
  const it = ITEMS[i];
  const rot = [-0.8, 0.9, -0.6, 0.8, -0.7][i];
  const w = 1090, h = 152;
  const y = ROW0 + i * GAP;
  const hw = tw(it.t, 52);
  const b = useBoil('pc' + i, 0.8, 0.25);
  return (
    <>
      <Land at={AT[i]} dur={9} x={1100} rot={7} style={{position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: 0}}>
        <div style={{transform: b}}>
          <Piece x={730} y={y} w={w} h={h} rot={rot} color={PAL.white} seed={`pc${i}`} amp={2.6} fiber={5} inner={{justifyContent: 'flex-start'}}>
            <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: 0, width: 128, height: h, overflow: 'hidden', borderRadius: '8px 0 0 8px'}}>
              <Piece x={0} y={0} w={128} h={h} color={it.c} seed={`pn${i}`} amp={2} fiber={0} shadow={false} pattern={i % 2 ? 'stripes' : 'dots'} patColor="#000">
                <div style={{fontFamily: DISP, fontSize: 92, color: it.tc, lineHeight: 1}}>{i + 1}</div>
              </Piece>
            </div>
            <div style={{marginLeft: 156, fontFamily: SERIF, fontWeight: 700, color: PAL.navy, lineHeight: 1.15, alignSelf: 'center', paddingTop: it.s ? 0 : 0}}>
              <div style={{fontSize: 52, whiteSpace: 'nowrap', WebkitTextStroke: `1.5px ${PAL.navy}`}}>{it.t}</div>
              {it.s && <div style={{fontSize: 30, fontFamily: SANS, fontWeight: 500, color: PAL.brown, marginTop: 14, whiteSpace: 'nowrap'}}>{it.s}</div>}
            </div>
            <Stroke d={underlineD(156, 156 + hw * 0.98, it.s ? 88 : 116, `pu${i}`)} at={cue(`pr.u${i}`)} dur={12} color={i % 2 ? PAL.teal : PAL.tomato} width={8} />
          </Piece>
          <Tape x={730 + 60 + i * 30} y={y - 16} rot={-5 + i * 2} color={`${it.c}bb`} />
        </div>
      </Land>
    </>
  );
};
```

**AXMORF** (mustard world): navy film strip (22 perforation holes top and bottom) whose four frames drop in on cues name/l1/l2/x1 (each frame = coloured paper + a tiny SVG picture), a clapperboard whose arm snaps shut on `t0`, a five-step pipeline row (场景 旁白 封面 校验 渲染) lit one by one, and a three-track timeline strip with coloured clips and a playhead (`play = prog(f, l2, end - l2)`). Full code:
*`reference-v3/src/scenes/Projects.tsx (Axmorf)`*
```tsx
const Axmorf: React.FC<V> = ({n, s}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame();
  const fr = [cu(n, 'name'), cu(n, 'l1'), cu(n, 'l2'), cu(n, 'x1')];
  const fc = [PAL.teal, PAL.tomato, PAL.pink, PAL.mustard];
  const clap = lp(f, cu(n, 't0'), 6, 0.16);
  const arm = -34 * (1 - Math.min(clap, 1.0)) + (clap > 1 ? (clap - 1) * -60 : 0);
  const play = prog(f, cu(n, 'l2'), cu(n, 'end') - cu(n, 'l2'));
  const steps = ['场景', '旁白', '封面', '校验', '渲染'];
  const stepAt = ['l2', 'x1', 't0', 't1', 't2'].map((k) => cu(n, k));
  const tracks = [
    {l: '场景', c: PAL.teal, clips: [[0, 0.33], [0.38, 0.7], [0.75, 1]]},
    {l: '旁白', c: PAL.tomato, clips: [[0.06, 0.5], [0.56, 0.94]]},
    {l: '封面', c: PAL.mustard, clips: [[0, 0.3]]},
  ];
  return (
    <>
      {/* film strip */}
      <Piece x={24} y={26} w={752} h={196} rot={-2} color={PAL.navy} seed="film" amp={2} fiber={4} tex>
        {Array.from({length: 22}).map((_, i) => (
          <React.Fragment key={i}>
            <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 14 + i * 34, top: 9, width: 18, height: 13, borderRadius: 3, background: PAL.cream}} />
            <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 14 + i * 34, bottom: 9, width: 18, height: 13, borderRadius: 3, background: PAL.cream}} />
          </React.Fragment>
        ))}
        {fr.map((at, i) => (
          <Land key={i} at={at} dur={8} y={-420} rot={-20} style={{position: 'absolute', left: 22 + i * 182, top: 40}}>
            <Piece x={0} y={0} w={160} h={116} color={fc[i]} seed={`fr${i}`} amp={1.6} fiber={3} shadow={false}>
              <svg width={160} height={116} style={{position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: 0}}>
                {i === 0 && <><circle cx={110} cy={36} r={18} fill={PAL.cream} /><polygon points="10,106 60,46 104,106" fill={PAL.navy} /></>}
                {i === 1 && <><rect x={30} y={30} width={100} height={56} rx={10} fill={PAL.cream} /><circle cx={80} cy={58} r={14} fill={PAL.tomato} /></>}
                {i === 2 && <><circle cx={60} cy={60} r={30} fill={PAL.white} /><circle cx={106} cy={50} r={18} fill={PAL.navy} /></>}
                {i === 3 && <><polygon points="80,18 120,98 40,98" fill={PAL.navy} /><circle cx={80} cy={70} r={10} fill={PAL.cream} /></>}
              </svg>
            </Piece>
          </Land>
        ))}
      </Piece>
      {/* clapperboard */}
      <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 440, top: 262, filter: SHADOW}}>
        <Piece x={0} y={50} w={290} h={196} rot={2} color={PAL.navy} seed="clap" amp={2} fiber={4} shadow={false}>
          <div style={{width: 230, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', gap: 16, alignItems: 'flex-start'}}>
            {[200, 150, 180].map((w, i) => <div key={i} style={{width: w, height: 13, borderRadius: 7, background: PAL.cream, opacity: 0.85}} />)}
          </div>
        </Piece>
        <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 4, top: 0, transform: `rotate(${arm}deg)`, transformOrigin: '0% 100%'}}>
          <Piece x={0} y={0} w={290} h={52} rot={0} color={PAL.cream} seed="arm" amp={1.4} fiber={3} shadow={false} inner={{display: 'block'}}>
            <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 8, top: 7, right: 8, bottom: 7, backgroundImage: `repeating-linear-gradient(115deg, ${PAL.ink} 0 26px, ${PAL.cream} 26px 52px)`, opacity: 0.92}} />
          </Piece>
        </div>
      </div>
      {/* timeline strip */}
      <Land at={cu(n, 'l1')} dur={9} x={-400} rot={-6} style={{position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: 0}}>
        <Piece x={24} y={262} w={392} h={214} rot={-1} color={PAL.white} seed="tlp" amp={2.4} fiber={4}>
          {tracks.map((t, ti) => (
            <div key={ti} style={{position: 'absolute', left: 16, top: 20 + ti * 62, width: 360, height: 48}}>
              <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: 6, fontFamily: SERIF, fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 26, color: PAL.navy}}>{t.l}</div>
              <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 78, top: 0, width: 280, height: 44, borderRadius: 8, background: 'rgba(120,90,50,0.1)'}}>
                {t.clips.map(([a, b], ci) => {
                  const p = ease(prog(f, cu(n, 'l1') + ti * 6 + ci * 5, 14));
                  return <div key={ci} style={{position: 'absolute', left: a * 280, width: (b - a) * 280 * p, top: 4, height: 36, borderRadius: 7, background: t.c, filter: 'url(#paperTex)'}} />;
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
          <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 94 + play * 262, top: 8, width: 5, height: 192, background: PAL.tomato, opacity: play > 0 && play < 1 ? 1 : 0}} />
        </Piece>
      </Land>
      {/* pipeline steps */}
      <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 50, top: 585, width: 700, height: 0, borderTop: `5px dashed ${PAL.kraft}`}} />
      {steps.map((st, i) => {
        const at = stepAt[i]; const on = f >= at; const last = i === 4;
        return (
          <Land key={i} at={at} dur={6} s={0.5} style={{position: 'absolute', left: 90 + i * 155 - 52, top: 536}}>
            <div style={{position: 'relative', width: 104, height: 104}}>
              <Shape pts={circlePts(52, 52, 44, 18)} color={on ? (last ? PAL.mustard : PAL.teal) : PAL.white} seed={`st${i}`} amp={3} fiber={4} />
              <Stroke d="M30 54 L46 70 L76 34" at={at} dur={8} color={on && last ? PAL.ink : PAL.white} width={10} />
              <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: -20, width: 144, top: 112, textAlign: 'center', fontFamily: SERIF, fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 30, color: PAL.navy}}>{st}</div>
            </div>
          </Land>
        );
      })}
    </>
  );
};
```

**Viselora** (teal world): three stacked paper layers = DOM / layout / WebGL, separated by shadow, with a floating tetrahedron lifting off the top layer:
*`reference-v3/src/scenes/Projects.tsx (Viselora)`*
```tsx
const Viselora: React.FC<V> = ({n, s}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame();
  const sheets = [
    {l: 'WEBGL', c: PAL.pink, y: 70, at: cu(n, 'l2'), pat: 'dots' as const, lc: PAL.tomato},
    {l: 'LAYOUT', c: PAL.teal, y: 270, at: cu(n, 'l1'), pat: 'grid' as const, lc: PAL.navy},
    {l: 'DOM', c: PAL.white, y: 470, at: cu(n, 'name'), pat: undefined, lc: PAL.mustard},
  ];
  const tp = lp(f, cu(n, 'x1'), 10, 0.1);
  const float = Math.sin(f / 9) * 10;
  return (
    <>
      {sheets.map((sh, i) => (
        <Land key={i} at={sh.at} dur={10} x={i % 2 ? 600 : -600} y={-60} rot={i % 2 ? 10 : -10} style={{position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: 0}}>
          <Piece x={120} y={sh.y} w={540} h={210} rot={-3} tf="skewX(-14deg)" color={sh.c} seed={`vs${i}`} amp={2.6} fiber={5} pattern={sh.pat} patColor={i === 1 ? '#fff' : '#a02040'} inner={{display: 'block'}}>
            {i === 2 && (
              <>
                <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 30, top: 24, width: 480, height: 30, borderRadius: 6, background: PAL.navy}} />
                {[0, 1, 2].map((q) => <div key={q} style={{position: 'absolute', left: 30 + q * 164, top: 74, width: 152, height: 100, borderRadius: 8, background: [PAL.mustard, PAL.tomato, PAL.teal][q], filter: 'url(#paperTex)'}} />)}
              </>
            )}
            {i === 1 && [0, 1, 2].map((q) => <div key={q} style={{position: 'absolute', left: 36 + q * 160, top: 40 + (q % 2) * 40, width: 140, height: 100, border: `5px dashed ${PAL.cream}`, borderRadius: 8}} />)}
          </Piece>
          <Piece x={96} y={sh.y + 150} w={tw(sh.l, 34) + 40} h={60} rot={-5} color={sh.lc} seed={`vl${i}`} amp={1.6} fiber={3}>
            <div style={{fontFamily: SANS, fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 34, color: i === 2 ? PAL.ink : PAL.white, letterSpacing: 3}}>{sh.l}</div>
          </Piece>
        </Land>
      ))}
      {[0, 1].map((i) => <Stroke key={i} d={`M 640 ${470 - i * 200 - 10} C 700 ${420 - i * 200}, 700 ${340 - i * 200}, 650 ${300 - i * 200}`} at={cu(n, i ? 't1' : 't0')} dur={9} color={PAL.navy} width={7} />)}
      {[0, 1].map((i) => <Stroke key={'h' + i} d={`M 650 ${300 - i * 200} l 6 28 M 650 ${300 - i * 200} l 30 -14`} at={cu(n, i ? 't1' : 't0') + 7} dur={3} color={PAL.navy} width={7} />)}
      <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 190, top: -110 + float, width: 420, height: 420, opacity: tp > 0.001 ? 1 : 0, transform: `translateY(${-(1 - tp) * 700}px) rotate(${(1 - tp) * 40}deg)`, filter: SHADOW}}>
        <Origami size={420} rotY={f * 0.03} rotX={0.5} />
      </div>
      {[0, 1, 2, 3].map((i) => {
        const p = lp(f, cu(n, 't2') + i * 2, 8, 0.1);
        const a = (i / 4) * Math.PI * 2 + 0.5;
        return <div key={i} style={{position: 'absolute', left: 400 + Math.cos(a) * 250 * Math.min(p, 1), top: 60 + Math.sin(a) * 140 * Math.min(p, 1), opacity: p > 0.001 && p < 1.05 ? 1 : 0}}><Star size={46} color={[PAL.mustard, PAL.tomato, PAL.teal, PAL.pink][i]} /></div>;
      })}
    </>
  );
};
```

**SyringeMeter** (pink/teal): paper camera icon (flashes when the shot is taken), a syringe whose red liquid fills over 63 frames (`fill = ease(prog(f, l1, 63))`), a dial whose needle sweeps -125 -> +40 deg with the fill, a curve that draws, and a CSV sheet that appears on the tag cues. No numbers are printed on the dial/curve, so nothing is invented:
*`reference-v3/src/scenes/Projects.tsx (Syringe)`*
```tsx
const Syringe: React.FC<V> = ({n, s}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame();
  const fill = ease(prog(f, cu(n, 'l1'), 63));
  const shot = decay(f, cu(n, 'l1'), 4);
  const curveD = smooth(Array.from({length: 14}, (_, i) => { const x = i / 13; return [20 + x * 350, 220 - Math.min(1, (x / 0.85) ** 1.1) * 170 + Math.sin(x * 20) * 4] as [number, number]; }));
  const csv = ['t0', 't1', 't2'].map((k) => cu(n, k));
  const needle = -125 + fill * 165;
  return (
    <>
      <Land at={cu(n, 'name')} dur={9} x={-500} rot={-20} style={{position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: 0}}>
        <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 14, top: 12}}><CamIcon size={190} flash={shot} /></div>
      </Land>
      <Stroke d="M 210 120 C 240 100, 250 110, 275 140" at={cu(n, 'l1')} dur={8} color={PAL.tomato} width={8} />
      <Stroke d="M 275 140 l -26 -2 M 275 140 l -6 -26" at={cu(n, 'l1') + 6} dur={3} color={PAL.tomato} width={8} />
      {/* syringe */}
      <Land at={cu(n, 'l1')} dur={9} x={500} rot={8} style={{position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: 0}}>
        <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 250, top: 100, transform: 'rotate(-4deg)', filter: SHADOW}}>
          <svg width={540} height={130} style={{overflow: 'visible', position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: 0}}>
            <polygon points="0,52 60,44 60,60" fill={PAL.kraft} stroke={PAL.fiber} strokeWidth={3} />
            <rect x={56} y={36} width={36} height={32} fill={PAL.navy} />
          </svg>
          <Piece x={90} y={0} w={420} h={104} color={PAL.white} seed="syb" amp={1.8} fiber={4} shadow={false} />
          <Piece x={96} y={8} w={Math.max(10, fill * 408)} h={88} color={PAL.tomato} seed="syl" amp={1.6} fiber={0} shadow={false} pattern="stripes" patColor="#fff" />
          {Array.from({length: 11}).map((_, i) => <div key={i} style={{position: 'absolute', left: 124 + i * 36, top: 6, width: 4, height: i % 5 === 0 ? 40 : 22, background: PAL.ink, borderRadius: 2, opacity: 0.8}} />)}
          <Piece x={96 + fill * 408} y={4} w={18} h={96} color={PAL.navy} seed="syp" amp={1.2} fiber={0} shadow={false} />
          <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 114 + fill * 408, top: 44, width: 110, height: 22, background: PAL.navy}} />
          <Piece x={224 + fill * 408} y={10} w={22} h={84} color={PAL.navy} seed="syt" amp={1.4} fiber={2} shadow={false} />
        </div>
      </Land>
      {/* dial */}
      <Land at={cu(n, 'l2')} dur={9} y={500} rot={-8} style={{position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: 0}}>
        <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 30, top: 290, width: 300, height: 300}}>
          <Shape pts={circlePts(150, 150, 140, 28)} color={PAL.white} seed="dial" amp={3.5} fiber={6} />
          <svg width={300} height={300} style={{position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: 0}}>
            <g filter="url(#paperTex)">
              <path d="M 40 190 A 112 112 0 0 1 100 52" stroke={PAL.teal} strokeWidth={26} fill="none" />
              <path d="M 108 48 A 112 112 0 0 1 192 48" stroke={PAL.mustard} strokeWidth={26} fill="none" />
              <path d="M 200 52 A 112 112 0 0 1 260 190" stroke={PAL.tomato} strokeWidth={26} fill="none" />
            </g>
            {Array.from({length: 13}).map((_, i) => { const a = (-215 + i * 15.8) * Math.PI / 180; return <line key={i} x1={150 + Math.cos(a) * 82} y1={150 + Math.sin(a) * 82} x2={150 + Math.cos(a) * 96} y2={150 + Math.sin(a) * 96} stroke={PAL.ink} strokeWidth={4} strokeLinecap="round" />; })}
            <g transform={`rotate(${needle} 150 160)`}><polygon points="150,160 142,166 70,160 142,154" fill={PAL.ink} /><polygon points="150,160 142,166 70,160 142,154" fill={PAL.ink} /></g>
            <circle cx={150} cy={160} r={14} fill={PAL.tomato} stroke={PAL.fiber} strokeWidth={4} />
          </svg>
        </div>
        <Label x={86} y={598} text="容量读数" at={cu(n, 'l2') + 4} font={SERIF} color={PAL.navy} />
      </Land>
      {/* curve on graph paper */}
      <Land at={cu(n, 'x1')} dur={9} x={500} rot={8} style={{position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: 0}}>
        <Piece x={370} y={290} w={400} h={260} rot={1.6} color={PAL.white} seed="gp" amp={2.4} fiber={5} pattern="grid" patColor="#1c6c80" inner={{display: 'block'}}>
          <Stroke d={curveD} at={cu(n, 'x1')} dur={cu(n, 'end') - cu(n, 'x1')} color={PAL.tomato} width={10} />
          <Stroke d="M 18 232 H 384 M 18 232 V 30" at={cu(n, 'x1')} dur={10} color={PAL.navy} width={5} />
        </Piece>
        <Label x={398} y={540} text="曲线" at={cu(n, 'x1') + 3} font={SERIF} color={PAL.teal} rot={2} />
      </Land>
      {/* CSV sheet */}
      <Land at={cu(n, 't0')} dur={9} y={400} rot={10} style={{position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: 0}}>
        <Piece x={470} y={596} w={300} h={104} rot={-3} color={PAL.cream2} seed="csv" amp={2.4} fiber={5} pattern="stripes" patColor="#6a8" inner={{display: 'block'}}>
          <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 16, top: 10, fontFamily: DISP, fontSize: 40, color: PAL.navy}}>CSV</div>
          {csv.map((at, i) => {
            const p = lp(f, at, 5, 0.1);
            return <div key={i} style={{position: 'absolute', left: 120, top: 14 + i * 26, display: 'flex', gap: 8, opacity: p > 0.01 ? 1 : 0, transform: `translateX(${(1 - p) * 40}px)`}}><div style={{width: 60, height: 14, borderRadius: 7, background: PAL.teal}} /><div style={{width: 80, height: 14, borderRadius: 7, background: PAL.mustard}} /></div>;
          })}
        </Piece>
      </Land>
    </>
  );
};
```

**vibe-journal-pipeline** (navy): journal pages sliding into a timeline:
*`reference-v3/src/scenes/Projects.tsx (Vibe)`*
```tsx
const Vibe: React.FC<V> = ({n, s}) => {
```

The shared per-project frame (name tile, two description lines, three tags, and which diorama to mount) is `Beat`:
*`reference-v3/src/scenes/Projects.tsx (Beat)`*
```tsx
const Beat: React.FC<{i: number}> = ({i}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame();
  const s = PROJ[i]; const n = i + 1; const p = PROJECTS[i];
  const Viz = p.Viz;
  const k = kickPulse(f, 6);
  const y0 = 170 + 168 * p.name.length + 40;
  return (
    <AbsoluteFill>
      <Layer depth={0.28}>
        <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 900, top: 20, transform: `rotate(${[-4, 5, -3, 4][i]}deg) scale(${lp(f, s + 6, 16, 0.04)})`, transformOrigin: '500px 480px'}}>
          <Shape pts={rectPts(1060, 960)} color={p.bg} seed={`bd${i}`} amp={9} fiber={10} pattern={i % 2 ? 'stripes' : 'dots'} patColor="#000" />
        </div>
        <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: -140, top: 780, transform: 'rotate(8deg)'}}><Shape pts={[[0, 360], [260, 0], [560, 360]]} color={PAL.kraft} seed={`bt${i}`} amp={7} fiber={7} /></div>
      </Layer>
      <Layer depth={0.6}>
        <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 840 - (f - s) * 0.6, top: 880, transform: `rotate(${f * 0.6}deg)`}}><Star size={86} color={p.accent} /></div>
      </Layer>
      <Layer depth={1}>
        <Land at={s + 9} dur={8} y={-50} style={{position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: 0}}>
          <Piece x={100} y={70} w={420} h={62} rot={-2} color={PAL.navy} seed={`pl${i}`} amp={2.4} fiber={4}>
            <div style={{fontFamily: SANS, fontWeight: 600, fontSize: 30, letterSpacing: 5, color: PAL.cream}}>PROJECT 0{n} / 04</div>
          </Piece>
          <Tape x={90} y={58} rot={-32} />
        </Land>
        {p.name.map(([t, sz, c, tc], q) => <NameTile key={q} t={t} size={sz} c={c} tc={tc} at={s + 9 + q * 3} i={q} />)}
        {p.zh.map((l, q) => (
          <Land key={q} at={q === 0 ? cu(n, 'l1') : cu(n, 'l2')} dur={9} x={-1000} rot={-4} style={{position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: 0}}>
            <Piece x={112} y={y0 + q * 86} w={tw(l, 40) + 60} h={72} rot={q ? 0.7 : -0.6} color={PAL.white} seed={`zh${i}${q}`} amp={2.2} fiber={4}>
              <div style={{fontFamily: SERIF, fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 40, color: PAL.navy, whiteSpace: 'nowrap'}}>{l}</div>
            </Piece>
          </Land>
        ))}
        {p.note && (
          <Land at={cu(n, 'l2')} dur={7} s={0.6} style={{position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: 0}}>
            <Piece x={120} y={y0 + 98} w={tw(p.note, 56) + 80} h={96} rot={-2} color={PAL.mustard} seed="note" amp={3} fiber={5} pattern="stripes" patColor="#000">
              <div style={{fontFamily: SERIF, fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 56, color: PAL.ink, WebkitTextStroke: `2px ${PAL.ink}`, whiteSpace: 'nowrap'}}>{p.note}</div>
            </Piece>
            <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 120, top: y0 + 98, transform: 'rotate(-2deg)'}}><Stroke d={underlineD(10, tw(p.note, 56) + 70, 90, 'nt')} at={cu(n, 'l2') + 4} dur={10} color={PAL.tomato} width={9} /></div>
          </Land>
        )}
        {p.tags.map(([t, c, tc], q) => {
          const at = cu(n, ['t0', 't1', 't2'][q]);
          const ty = (p.note ? y0 + 232 : y0 + p.zh.length * 86 + 36);
          const tx = 120 + q * 0 + p.tags.slice(0, q).reduce((a, tg) => a + tw(tg[0], 32) + 100, 0);
          return (
            <Land key={q} at={at} dur={6} s={0.7} rot={-12} style={{position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: 0}}>
              <Piece x={tx} y={ty} w={tw(t, 32) + 56} h={62} rot={[-3, 2, -2][q]} color={c} seed={`tg${i}${q}`} amp={2} fiber={4}>
                <div style={{fontFamily: SANS, fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 32, color: tc, whiteSpace: 'nowrap'}}>{t}</div>
              </Piece>
            </Land>
          );
        })}
        {/* diorama stage */}
        <Land at={s + 9} dur={11} x={1000} rot={8} style={{position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: 0}}>
          <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 1030, top: 150, width: 800, height: 740}}>
            <Piece x={0} y={0} w={800} h={740} rot={1} color={PAL.kraft} seed={`stg${i}`} amp={4} fiber={7} pattern="stripes" patColor="#6a4a20" inner={{display: 'block'}}>
              <Boil k={`dio${i}`} amp={1.2} rot={0.3} style={{position: 'absolute', inset: 0}}>
                <Viz n={n} s={s} />
              </Boil>
            </Piece>
            <Tape x={20} y={-14} rot={-35} w={170} />
            <Tape x={640} y={-14} rot={32} w={170} />
          </div>
        </Land>
        {/* progress segments */}
        <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 112, bottom: 70, display: 'flex', gap: 14}}>
          {PROJECTS.map((q, qi) => (
            <div key={qi} style={{position: 'relative', width: 120, height: 22}}>
              <Piece x={0} y={0} w={120} h={22} color={qi < i || qi === i ? q.accent : PAL.cream2} seed={`pr${qi}`} amp={1.6} fiber={3} shadow={qi <= i} />
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </Layer>
    </AbsoluteFill>
  );
};
```

**Links**: three postcards flying up (each with an icon, name, URL, circled with a hand loop); **Outro**: nine tiles slam "zzzxc.com" (3-frame stagger), robot waves, confetti burst (30 pieces) on the final stamp, kick pulse on tile scale. See the appendix.

## 5.10 Transitions
Only one transition type was implemented in v3: the **torn-paper wipe** (`Overlay` in `Camera.tsx`, printed in 04-sync.md §4.5): three oversized rough-cut sheets (2700x1200, `amp=30`) in kraft / accent / accent, sliding right-to-left (4700 px over 18 frames, `lead = 1960 - ((f - s)/18) * 4700`, start `s = cut - 13 + 2i`), tilted -0.6/0/+0.6 deg, with halftone dots or stripes on the middle/top sheets, + a white flash (0.4, decay 2.2 f, capped at 0.5) = camera shutter. The colour triple differs per cut (`WIPE[ci]`). The scene underneath is swapped on the cut frame while the sheets cover the screen. I did not build paper-flip/fold transitions (the brief mentioned them; the wipe + landings were enough and cheaper).

## 5.11 The critique loop (what I really did) and before/after examples
Loop: build scene -> `remotion still` at (a) an in-between frame (b) the "fully built" frame -> look at the image at 100 % -> write down defects -> fix -> re-render that still. Contact sheets of 4-6 stills at 960x540 for overall consistency. Check: Chinese glyphs, overflow/clipping, overlaps, contrast, "does it look hand-made", hierarchy.

| # | defect seen in the still | fix (real values) | images |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Title: mustard tetrahedron on mustard disc: the origami vanished into its background; paper texture too dark/blotchy (looks like wood) | disc colour mustard -> navy; tetra rotation start/speed changed (`1.0 + f*0.024`) so faces never go edge-on; `paperTex` matrix `0.55 -> 0.42` and offset `0.78 -> 0.86` (less contrast, lighter) | `images/before_title_f40.jpg` / `images/after_title_f55.jpg` |
| 2 | Intro: "我是" tile too narrow so it wrapped to two lines ("我" / "是"); label "01 / ABOUT" collided with it; the underline struck through the sub-text | tile width up; label moved; underline y re-measured | `images/before_intro_f270.jpg` / `images/after_intro_f250.jpg` |
| 3 | Principles: marker underline ran through the grey sub-line; counter badge showed "00" before the first card | underline y for cards with sub-line = 88 (without = 116); badge opacity = `lp(f, AT[0], 10)` | `images/before_principles_f470.jpg` / `images/after_principles_f400.jpg` |
| 4 | Outro: confetti hid the tagline | 44 -> 30 pieces, upward/outward arcs | `images/v3_outro_f1130.jpg` |
| 5 | Outro: the "." tile looked like an "l" | plain tomato circle dot aligned to baseline | |
| 6 | v2 (dark style) examples of the same kind: flash at impact washed out the frame (0.85 -> 0.6, decay 3.5 -> 2.2); HUD scaled by camera zoom (moved outside `Camera`); dimmed rows too faint (0.45 -> 0.55); link cards overflowed (width 560 -> 540, left 100 -> 130, gap 50 -> 40, press 0.05 -> 0.025) | | |

### Pre-delivery critique checklist (my own, heuristic)
1. Can I read every word in a still? (size >= 26 px, contrast >= 4.5 or large bold >= 3)
2. Is there one obvious hero per scene? Is the eye path left->right / top->bottom?
3. Does anything touch/overlap unintentionally (tiles, underline vs text, labels vs tiles)?
4. Is the edge safe at max camera zoom + pan?
5. Does it look hand-made: rough edges, tilt, shadow, texture, jitter, no perfect circles/straight lines?
6. Same palette, same fonts, same shadows in all scenes?
7. Does each hit have a visual landing on its SFX frame (step through n-1, n, n+1)?
8. Is something still moving while the viewer should be reading?


---

# 6. Starter template (`starter/`) - verified working

A complete miniature of the v3 pipeline: 3 bars at 100 BPM = **216 frames = 7.2 s**, 1920x1080 @ 30 fps, two scenes, one torn-paper wipe, 22 cues, 78 music events. It uses the real `paper.tsx`, `icons.tsx`, `Camera.tsx`, `Bg.tsx`, `timeline.ts`, `theme.ts`, `synth_audio.py`, `master.sh` copied unchanged from v3. New for the starter: `tools/make_timeline.py` (short version), `src/Root.tsx`, `src/Video.tsx` (generic scene list + optional debug overlay), `src/scenes/Open.tsx`, `src/scenes/Main.tsx`, `tools/check_sync.py`, `run.sh`.

## 6.1 Layout
```
starter/
  package.json, tsconfig.json, remotion.config.ts      (Remotion 4.0.534, React 19, jpeg q92)
  run.sh                                               one-command build + checks
  tools/make_timeline.py                               single source of truth -> src/timeline.json
  tools/synth_audio.py                                 numpy synth: music + SFX (reads timeline.json)
  tools/master.sh                                      two-pass loudnorm -> public/audio.wav
  tools/check_sync.py                                  RMS / low-band step at every cue in the final mp4
  src/index.ts, Root.tsx, Video.tsx                    composition, audio, camera, global filters, fade, wipes, grain
  src/timeline.ts                                      cue(), lp(), decay(), kickPulse(), IMPACTS, inScene()
  src/theme.ts, paper.tsx, icons.tsx, Bg.tsx, Camera.tsx
  src/scenes/Open.tsx                                  cold open: 3 strips land on beats, origami, riser into the cut
  src/scenes/Main.tsx                                  after the cut: "starter" tiles, 3 cards + arrows, tagline, DONE stamp
```

## 6.2 How to run
```bash
cd /workspace/video-playbook/starter
# node_modules is excluded from the zip; first time: npm install   (needs network; ~1 min)
./run.sh                 # CONC=7 ./run.sh to set render concurrency
```
`run.sh` = `make_timeline.py` -> `synth_audio.py` -> `master.sh` -> `npx tsc` -> `remotion render` (crf 22, AAC 192k) -> ffprobe -> ebur128 -> `check_sync.py`. Output: `out/sample.mp4`. Single frame preview: `npx remotion still src/index.ts Starter stills/f100.png --frame=100`. Requirements: Node 18+, Python 3 + numpy, ffmpeg/ffprobe, system fonts (Noto Serif CJK SC, Abril Fatface, Rubik; others fall back).

To adapt: edit `scenes`, `cues`, `music` in `make_timeline.py`; add scene components keyed by the same scene names in `Video.tsx`; use `cue('scene.thing')` for every landing.

## 6.3 Verified results (from the real run in this environment)
* Render: 216 frames, ~56 s wall time for the whole `run.sh` (7 workers), `out/sample.mp4` 16.9 MB at crf 22 (not kept in the zip; `run.sh` recreates it) (grain is expensive), re-encoded to `starter-sample.mp4` **4.3 MB** with `-crf 27 -preset slow` (audio stream copied).
* `ffprobe starter-sample.mp4`: `h264 1920x1080 30 fps duration 7.2 s` + `aac duration 7.21 s`.
* Loudness of the sample: **-14.5 LUFS, peak -2.2 dBFS** (short clip; v3 full video was -14.2 LUFS, TP -1.6 dBTP).
* `check_sync.py` on the render: stamps, riser/shutter/wipe, boing, slaps, ding all show RMS jumps 2.4x-4.8x exactly at their cue frames (full table in 04-sync.md §4.6).
* Stills: ![starter frames 60, 100, 150, 195](images/starter_sheet.jpg)

## 6.4 What to change first when making your own video
1. `theme.ts` palette and fonts; 2. `make_timeline.py` scenes/cues/music; 3. replace `Open.tsx`/`Main.tsx` text, keep the `Land`/`Piece`/`Stroke`/`Stamp` structure; 4. `WIPE` colours in `Camera.tsx` (one triple per cut; it is indexed by cut number, so add entries if you add cuts - the starter has 1 cut, v3 has 8); 5. end fade: `TOTAL - 22` in `Video.tsx`.


---

# 7. Checklist: the difference between "works" and "looks good"

I believe the following are the details most likely to be missed. **Bold = I saw or fixed this in the real project.**

## Sync
- [ ] **BPM gives an integer frames-per-beat** (100 BPM -> 18). Everything (cues, cuts, music steps) is a multiple of 9 f.
- [ ] **One JSON feeds both audio and picture; every landing uses `cue()`, never a typed frame number.**
- [ ] **SFX peak, not start, is on the cue frame** (whoosh/slide/riser use peak offsets).
- [ ] Piece is at rest ON the cue frame (`lp` returns 1.0 there); step n-1, n, n+1 stills.
- [ ] **Every cut has shutter + wipe SFX peaking on the cut frame; first landing after a cut is cut + 9.**
- [ ] **Riser ends exactly on the drop frame.**
- [ ] Re-run `make_timeline` -> `synth` -> `master` -> render after ANY timeline change.
- [ ] Stamp power and slap cues also drive camera punch (one place: `IMPACTS`).

## Audio
- [ ] **Loudness: -14 LUFS, true peak <= -1.5 dBTP measured AFTER AAC encoding (set `TP=-2.7` in loudnorm).**
- [ ] **Music is sparse where SFX land** (plucks decay fast, melody leaves 8th rests, no sustained mid-range pad over hits).
- [ ] Cold open is sparse, the drop adds kick+bass+chucks; the ending drops to almost nothing, then one chord on the final hit.
- [ ] 1.2 s fade at the end; picture fades to paper colour 20 f before the end.
- [ ] Vary SFX per element type and use per-cue `vol`; avoid 4 identical sounds in a row.

## Visual: the 15 details that make it look designed
1. **Real paper edges**: roughPoly on EVERY shape + cream fibre rim; never use a bare `<div>` background for a piece.
2. **Texture on top of colour**, not instead of it (multiply blend, mild: noise contrast 0.42, offset 0.86; strong noise makes it look dirty).
3. **Warm brown shadows (two layers: tight + soft)**, never black.
4. **Global grain + vignette + fibres + crease** on the base paper.
5. **Rotation +-0.5-4 deg and 14 px y-offset alternation** on tiles/strips; nothing perfectly aligned.
6. **Boil**: jitter stepped every 3 f (<= 1.6 px, <= 0.8 deg) on some layers, smooth motion on the rest; whole film re-seeded displacement every 3 f.
7. **Three parallax layers** (0.25 / 0.55 / 1.0) + slow push-in 1.02 -> 1.05 + alternating pan direction per scene.
8. **Landings with ease-out + 7 % overshoot** (`lp`), 6-10 frames long; stamps come from 1.7-2.1x scale.
9. **Anticipation by sound**: riser before drops; tape/paper rustle just before big landings.
10. **Hand-drawn annotations** (circle, arrow, underline) that draw on AFTER the object lands (+4..+12 f).
11. **One dominant hue per scene** and a consistent neutral base (~60 % paper).
12. **Type thick enough**: same-colour `WebkitTextStroke` 1.5-4 px on CJK Bold; hierarchy ratio ~1:2:4:8.
13. **Large text on saturated colour, small text on navy/cream only** (white on tomato = 3.6:1).
14. **Safe margins >= 100 px** for camera drift/zoom; test the final frame of each scene.
15. **Fonts verified in the first still** (CJK glyphs, fallback). Manual line breaks. Measure widths with an estimator and re-check after copy edits.

## Process
- [ ] **Stills for every scene at the "built" frame AND at one mid-animation frame before the first full render.**
- [ ] **Hooks never in conditionals/maps with variable counts**; wrap in a tiny component.
- [ ] **`npx tsc` clean, 30-frame test render before a 7-minute full render.**
- [ ] **`cd` to the project root in every command chain** (stills landed in `src/stills/` once).
- [ ] Render at crf 17 once, then re-encode with ffmpeg (crf 22 full, crf 27 + hqdn3d mobile); grain makes direct high-quality renders huge (234 MB).
- [ ] Verify with ffprobe (codec, size, fps, duration), ebur128, sync report, frame grabs at cues.
- [ ] Only real content on screen; no invented numbers.
- [ ] Reading time: new sentences >= ~2.5-3 s for 16 CJK chars; labels >= 0.8-1 s. **(v3 violated this for project descriptions/tags and the title sub-lines; do better.)**
- [ ] Say honestly in the report what you could not check (listening).

## Things I'd improve next time (honest)
* Longer holds for project descriptions (currently 1.7-2.3 s for 17-19 chars).
* Real SFX ducking against the pad/bass (v2 had it; v3 relied on arrangement).
* A debug overlay and an automated per-cue check were not part of v1-v3; the starter adds them.
* Paper-flip/fold transitions were requested but not implemented.


---

# Appendix A. Full real v3 source (copied from /workspace/zzzxc-remotion-v3)

## A: `tools/make_timeline.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""v3 single source of truth -> src/timeline.json (beat grid, music events, SFX cues, scene cuts).
Read by tools/synth_audio.py (audio) AND src/timeline.ts (animation)."""
import json
FPS = 30; BPM = 100; BEAT = 18; BAR = 72; TOTAL = 1224   # 100 BPM: 18 frames/beat, 72/bar, 17 bars = 40.8 s
EIGHTH = BEAT / 2
scenes = {'title': [0, 144], 'intro': [144, 288], 'principles': [288, 504], 'projects': [504, 936], 'links': [936, 1080], 'outro': [1080, 1224]}
cuts = [144, 288, 504, 612, 720, 828, 936, 1080]
PROJ = [504, 612, 720, 828]
cues = []
def add(id, frame, type, **kw): cues.append(dict(id=id, frame=frame, type=type, **kw))

# ---- cold open (0-72): paper faces tape down, three phrase strips land on beats 2,3,4
for i, f in enumerate([0, 9, 27, 45]): add(f'open.f{i}', f, 'tape', vol=0.5)
add('open.s0', 18, 'slide', vol=0.7); add('open.s1', 36, 'slide', vol=0.7)
add('open.s2', 54, 'stamp', power=0.8, vol=0.9)
add('open.circle', 58, 'scribble', dur=14, vol=0.55)
add('open.riser', 72, 'riser', dur=1.2, vol=0.7)
# ---- title card
add('title.drop', 72, 'stamp', power=1.0, vol=1.0); add('title.boing', 81, 'boing', vol=0.55)
add('title.tag', 99, 'tape', vol=0.7); add('title.s0', 108, 'slide', vol=0.6); add('title.s1', 126, 'slide', vol=0.6)
# ---- cuts: shutter click on the cut frame + torn-paper wipe whoosh peaking on the cut
for c in cuts:
    add(f'shutter@{c}', c, 'shutter', vol=0.8)
    add(f'wipe@{c}', c, 'wipe', vol=0.75)
# ---- intro (cut 144; first landing on the 8th after the cut)
add('intro.l0', 153, 'stamp', power=0.6, vol=0.8); add('intro.l1', 171, 'slide', vol=0.65)
add('intro.l2', 189, 'stamp', power=0.7, vol=0.85); add('intro.u', 193, 'scribble', dur=12, vol=0.5)
add('intro.robot', 198, 'boing', vol=0.5)
add('intro.n0', 207, 'slap', vol=0.8); add('intro.a0', 216, 'scribble', dur=9, vol=0.45)
add('intro.n1', 225, 'slap', vol=0.8); add('intro.a1', 234, 'scribble', dur=9, vol=0.45)
add('intro.n2', 243, 'slap', vol=0.85)
add('intro.tag', 261, 'slide', vol=0.7); add('intro.hl', 265, 'scribble', dur=14, vol=0.5); add('intro.ding', 279, 'ding', vol=0.5)
# ---- principles
add('pr.head', 297, 'stamp', power=0.7, vol=0.85)
for i in range(5):
    add(f'pr.i{i}', 306 + 36 * i, 'slap', vol=0.8)
    add(f'pr.u{i}', 306 + 36 * i + 12, 'scribble', dur=12, vol=0.4)
add('pr.ding', 486, 'ding', vol=0.5)
# ---- projects (108 frames = 6 beats each)
for n, s in enumerate(PROJ, 1):
    add(f'p{n}.name', s + 9, 'stamp', power=0.7, vol=0.85)
    add(f'p{n}.l1', s + 27, 'slide', vol=0.55); add(f'p{n}.l2', s + 45, 'slide', vol=0.55)
    add(f'p{n}.x1', s + 54, 'tape', vol=0.5)
    add(f'p{n}.t0', s + 63, 'pop', vol=0.6); add(f'p{n}.t1', s + 72, 'pop', vol=0.6); add(f'p{n}.t2', s + 81, 'pop', vol=0.65)
    add(f'p{n}.end', s + 99, 'ding', vol=0.35)
# ---- links
add('ln.head', 945, 'stamp', power=0.6, vol=0.8)
for i in range(3):
    add(f'ln.c{i}', 963 + 18 * i, 'slap', vol=0.8)
    add(f'ln.o{i}', 972 + 18 * i, 'scribble', dur=9, vol=0.45)
add('ln.riser', 1080, 'riser', dur=1.2, vol=0.7)
# ---- outro
add('out.slam', 1089, 'stamp', power=1.0, vol=1.0); add('out.boing', 1098, 'boing', vol=0.5)
add('out.sub', 1107, 'slide', vol=0.65); add('out.t0', 1125, 'pop', vol=0.7); add('out.t1', 1134, 'pop', vol=0.7)
add('out.hl', 1140, 'scribble', dur=14, vol=0.5)
add('out.final', 1188, 'stamp', power=0.8, vol=0.9); add('out.ding', 1188, 'ding', vol=0.7)

# ------------------------------------------------------------ music events
ev = []
def e(inst, f, **kw): ev.append(dict(i=inst, f=round(f, 2), **kw))
CH = [dict(root=36, tones=[60, 64, 67], pad=[48, 55, 60, 64]), dict(root=31, tones=[59, 62, 67], pad=[43, 55, 59, 62]),
      dict(root=33, tones=[57, 60, 64], pad=[45, 52, 57, 60]), dict(root=29, tones=[57, 60, 65], pad=[41, 53, 57, 60])]   # C G Am F
MEL = [[(0, 79), (2, 76), (3, 79), (4, 84), (6, 79)], [(0, 83), (2, 79), (3, 74), (4, 79), (6, 83)],
       [(0, 81), (2, 76), (3, 72), (4, 76), (6, 81)], [(0, 77), (2, 81), (3, 84), (4, 81), (6, 77), (7, 72)]]
NBARS = 17
for bar in range(NBARS):
    b0 = bar * BAR; ch = CH[bar % 4]
    e('pad', b0, notes=ch['pad'], dur=BAR / FPS, atk=0.9 if bar == 0 else 0.25, v=0.8 if bar < 16 else 1.0)
    # shaker 8ths
    for st in range(8):
        f = b0 + st * EIGHTH
        if bar == 0 and f < 9: continue
        e('shaker', f, v=0.55 if st % 2 else 0.3)
# cold-open hook: pizzicato rising on the three phrase landings, woodblock tick, brushed build
for f, n in [(18, 72), (36, 76), (54, 79), (63, 84)]: e('pizz', f, note=n, v=0.9)
for f in [9, 27, 45, 63]: e('wood', f, v=0.5)
for i in range(4): e('brush', 54 + i * 4.5, v=0.25 + 0.15 * i)
for bar in range(1, NBARS):
    b0 = bar * BAR; ch = CH[bar % 4]; last = bar == 16
    if not last:
        for beat in (0, 2): e('kick', b0 + beat * BEAT, v=0.9)
        for beat in (1, 3): e('clap', b0 + beat * BEAT, v=0.45); e('brush', b0 + beat * BEAT, v=0.5)
        # bass: bounce
        for st, off in [(0, 0), (3, 7), (4, 12), (6, 7)]: e('bass', b0 + st * EIGHTH, note=ch['root'] + off, v=0.9)
        # ukulele chucks on off-8ths
        for st in (1, 3, 5, 7): e('uke', b0 + st * EIGHTH, notes=ch['tones'], v=0.7 if st in (3, 7) else 0.45, down=(st in (3, 7)))
    else:
        e('kick', b0, v=0.9); e('bass', b0, note=ch['root'], v=0.9)
    # melody: kalimba on odd bars, marimba on even bars
    if bar < 16:
        for st, n in MEL[bar % 4]: e('kalimba' if bar % 2 else 'marimba', b0 + st * EIGHTH, note=n, v=0.8)
    # pizzicato counter-line during the projects
    if 7 <= bar <= 12:
        for st in (0, 2, 5, 6): e('pizz', b0 + st * EIGHTH, note=ch['tones'][(st // 2) % 3] - 12, v=0.55)
# outro ending: single notes then a ta-da chord at 1188
e('kalimba', 1152, note=76, v=0.8); e('kalimba', 1170, note=79, v=0.8)
e('uke', 1188, notes=[48, 55, 60, 64, 67, 72], v=1.0, down=True)
e('bass', 1188, note=36, v=1.0)
for i, n in enumerate([72, 76, 79, 84]): e('marimba', 1188 + 2 + i * 3, note=n, v=0.7)
e('kick', 1188, v=0.9)

tl = dict(fps=FPS, bpm=BPM, beat=BEAT, bar=BAR, total=TOTAL, scenes=scenes, cuts=cuts, projects=PROJ,
          kicks=sorted(x['f'] for x in ev if x['i'] == 'kick'), cues=cues, music=ev)
json.dump(tl, open('src/timeline.json', 'w'), indent=0)
print('cues', len(cues), 'events', len(ev))
```

## A: `tools/synth_audio.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""v3: playful organic soundtrack + tactile paper SFX, synthesised with numpy only (no samples, no downloads).
Reads src/timeline.json; every event is placed at frame/fps seconds."""
import json, wave, sys, numpy as np
SR = 44100
rng = np.random.default_rng(11)
TL = json.load(open('src/timeline.json'))
FPS = TL['fps']; N = int(TL['total'] / FPS * SR) + SR * 3
T = lambda n: np.arange(int(n * SR)) / SR
mid = lambda m: 440.0 * 2 ** ((m - 69) / 12)
smp = lambda frame: int(round(frame / FPS * SR))
def noise(n): return rng.standard_normal(n)
def fft_filter(x, lo=None, hi=None):
    X = np.fft.rfft(x); f = np.fft.rfftfreq(len(x), 1 / SR); m = np.ones_like(f)
    if lo: m = m / (1 + (lo / np.maximum(f, 1)) ** 4)
    if hi: m = m / (1 + (f / hi) ** 4)
    return np.fft.irfft(X * m, len(x))
ex = lambda t, k: np.exp(-t * k)
def norm(x, peak=1.0): return x / (np.abs(x).max() + 1e-9) * peak
def stereo(x, pan=0.0):
    a = (pan + 1) * np.pi / 4; return np.stack([x * np.cos(a), x * np.sin(a)], 1)
def place(buf, sig, start, gain=1.0):
    if sig.ndim == 1: sig = stereo(sig)
    s = max(start, 0); off = s - start
    if s >= len(buf): return
    seg = sig[off:off + len(buf) - s]; buf[s:s + len(seg)] += seg * gain
def reverb(x, decay=0.8, mix=0.25):
    n = int(decay * SR); t = np.arange(n) / SR
    ir = np.stack([fft_filter(noise(n), 250, 6000) * np.exp(-t * 6 / decay) for _ in range(2)], 1) * 0.05
    xs = x if x.ndim == 2 else stereo(x); L = len(xs) + n; out = np.zeros((L, 2))
    for c in range(2): out[:, c] = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(xs[:, c], L) * np.fft.rfft(ir[:, c], L), L)
    base = np.zeros((L, 2)); base[:len(xs)] = xs
    return base * (1 - mix * 0.5) + out * mix * 2.0
def sweep_noise(dur, f0, f1, q=0.6):
    n = int(dur * SR); x = noise(n); win = 1024; hop = win // 2
    out = np.zeros(n + win); w = np.hanning(win); fr = np.fft.rfftfreq(win, 1 / SR)
    for s in range(0, n - win, hop):
        p = s / n; fc = f0 * (f1 / f0) ** p
        m = np.exp(-0.5 * (np.log2(np.maximum(fr, 20) / fc) / q) ** 2)
        out[s:s + win] += np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(x[s:s + win] * w) * m, win) * w
    return out[:n]

# ------------------------------------------------------------------ instruments
def partials(t, f, parts, amps, decs):
    x = np.zeros(len(t))
    for p, a, d in zip(parts, amps, decs):
        if f * p < 15000: x += a * np.sin(2 * np.pi * f * p * t + rng.uniform(0, .5)) * ex(t, d)
    return x
def pizz(note, v=1.0):
    t = T(0.5); f = mid(note); x = np.zeros(len(t))
    for h in range(1, 9):
        if f * h > 12000: break
        x += np.sin(2 * np.pi * f * h * t) / h ** 1.2 * ex(t, 9 + 7 * h)
    x += fft_filter(noise(len(t)), 1500, 6000) * ex(t, 400) * 0.25
    return stereo(norm(x, 0.5) * v, pan=0.15)
def kalimba(note, v=1.0):
    t = T(1.1); f = mid(note)
    x = partials(t, f, [1, 5.4, 9.8], [1, .22, .08], [3.2, 14, 30]) + fft_filter(noise(len(t)), 2000, 7000) * ex(t, 300) * 0.12
    return stereo(norm(x, 0.5) * v, pan=-0.2)
def marimba(note, v=1.0):
    t = T(0.8); f = mid(note)
    x = partials(t, f, [1, 3.95, 9.2], [1, .45, .07], [5.5, 20, 45]) + fft_filter(noise(len(t)), 300, 1500) * ex(t, 120) * 0.2
    return stereo(norm(x, 0.55) * v, pan=0.25)
def bass(note, v=1.0):
    t = T(0.55); f = mid(note)
    x = partials(t, f, [1, 2, 3, 4], [1, .55, .22, .1], [4.5, 7, 10, 14]) + fft_filter(noise(len(t)), 100, 600) * ex(t, 160) * 0.3
    return stereo(np.tanh(norm(x, 0.8) * 1.2) * 0.6 * v)
def uke(notes, v=1.0, down=True):
    t = T(0.55); out = np.zeros((len(t), 2))
    for i, m in enumerate(notes if down else notes[::-1]):
        f = mid(m); x = np.zeros(len(t))
        for h in range(1, 8):
            if f * h < 9000: x += np.sin(2 * np.pi * f * h * t) / h ** 1.1 * ex(t, 12 + 6 * h)
        d = int(i * 0.007 * SR); sig = np.concatenate([np.zeros(d), x])[:len(t)]
        out += stereo(sig * 0.2, pan=-0.35 + i * 0.12)
    out += stereo(fft_filter(noise(len(t)), 1800, 6500) * ex(t, 160) * 0.08)
    return out * v * 0.9
def pad(notes, dur, atk, v=1.0):
    n = int((dur + 0.7) * SR); t = np.arange(n) / SR; out = np.zeros((n, 2))
    a = np.minimum(1, t / atk) ** 1.4; r = np.where(t < dur, 1, np.exp(-(t - dur) * 6))
    for m in notes:
        for j, d in enumerate((-0.004, 0.004)):
            f = mid(m) * (1 + d); x = np.sin(2 * np.pi * f * t) + 0.25 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 2 * f * t) * (1 + 0.3 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 0.25 * t))
            out += stereo(x * 0.05, pan=(j - .5) * 1.2)
    return out * (a * r)[:, None] * v
def shaker(v=1.0):
    t = T(0.09); x = fft_filter(noise(len(t)), lo=5500) * np.minimum(1, t / 0.004) * ex(t, 45)
    return stereo(norm(x, 0.22) * v, pan=0.35)
def brush(v=1.0):
    t = T(0.22); x = fft_filter(noise(len(t)), 1800, 8000) * (np.minimum(1, t / 0.03)) * ex(t, 14)
    return stereo(norm(x, 0.28) * v, pan=-0.2)
def kickb(v=1.0):
    t = T(0.4); f = 52 + 70 * ex(t, 40); x = np.sin(2 * np.pi * np.cumsum(f) / SR) * ex(t, 11)
    x += fft_filter(noise(len(t)), 100, 500) * ex(t, 120) * 0.15
    return stereo(np.tanh(x * 1.2) * 0.8 * v)
def clap(v=1.0):
    t = T(0.25); x = np.zeros(len(t))
    for d in (0, .009): x += np.concatenate([np.zeros(int(d * SR)), (fft_filter(noise(len(t)), 900, 3200) * ex(t, 120))[:len(t) - int(d * SR)]])
    x += fft_filter(noise(len(t)), 900, 3000) * ex(t, 22) * 0.4
    return stereo(norm(x, 0.4) * v, pan=0.1)
def wood(v=1.0):
    t = T(0.12); x = np.sin(2 * np.pi * 1250 * t) * ex(t, 60) + 0.4 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 2300 * t) * ex(t, 90)
    return stereo(norm(x, 0.35) * v, pan=0.3)
def bell(f, v=1.0, d=1.0):
    t = T(1.8); x = partials(t, f, [1, 2.76, 5.4, 8.93], [1, .5, .25, .12], [2.6 / d, 4 / d, 7, 10])
    return stereo(norm(x, 0.5) * v)

# ------------------------------------------------------------------ SFX (return (signal, peak_offset_samples))
def s_slide(vol=0.7):
    d = 0.3; n = int(d * SR); t = np.arange(n) / SR
    x = fft_filter(noise(n), 700, 7000); crinkle = fft_filter(noise(n), hi=60); crinkle = 0.55 + 0.45 * norm(crinkle)
    e = np.sin(np.pi / 2 * np.minimum(t / (d * .8), 1)) ** 1.5 * np.where(t < d * .8, 1, np.exp(-(t - d * .8) * 70))
    y = x * e * crinkle; return stereo(norm(y, 0.45) * vol), int(d * .8 * SR)
def s_slap(vol=0.8):
    t = T(0.3); x = fft_filter(noise(len(t)), 300, 2500) * ex(t, 55) * 0.8 + np.sin(2 * np.pi * np.cumsum(140 * ex(t, 22) + 60) / SR) * ex(t, 22) * 0.7
    x += fft_filter(noise(len(t)), lo=3000) * ex(t, 250) * 0.25
    return reverb(stereo(norm(x, 0.6) * vol), 0.25, 0.12), 0
def s_stamp(power=1.0, vol=1.0):
    t = T(0.6); x = np.sin(2 * np.pi * np.cumsum(95 * ex(t, 25) + 42) / SR) * ex(t, 11) + fft_filter(noise(len(t)), 100, 900) * ex(t, 40) * 0.55
    x += fft_filter(noise(len(t)), lo=2500) * ex(t, 400) * 0.35
    y = reverb(stereo(np.tanh(x * 1.3)), 0.35, 0.15)
    return norm(y, 0.9) * vol * (0.55 + 0.45 * power), 0
def s_scribble(dur=12, vol=0.5):
    d = dur / FPS + 0.04; n = int(d * SR); t = np.arange(n) / SR
    x = fft_filter(noise(n), 1800, 6500)
    am = 0.55 + 0.45 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * (11 + 6 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 3 * t)) * t) ** 2
    e = np.minimum(1, t / 0.02) * np.minimum(1, (d - t) / 0.03)
    return stereo(norm(x * am * e, 0.35) * vol, pan=0.1), 0
def s_tape(vol=0.5):
    t = T(0.12); x = fft_filter(noise(len(t)), lo=3500) * ex(t, 180) * 0.8 + fft_filter(noise(len(t)), 1500, 5000) * np.minimum(1, t / 0.005) * ex(t, 30) * 0.35
    return stereo(norm(x, 0.35) * vol), 0
def s_pop(vol=0.6):
    t = T(0.14); f = 380 + 900 * (1 - ex(t, 45)); x = np.sin(2 * np.pi * np.cumsum(f) / SR) * ex(t, 40)
    x += fft_filter(noise(len(t)), lo=3000) * ex(t, 400) * 0.2
    return stereo(norm(x, 0.45) * vol), 0
def s_boing(vol=0.5):
    t = T(0.55); f = 300 + 220 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * 9 * t) * ex(t, 5) + 260 * ex(t, 6)
    x = np.sin(2 * np.pi * np.cumsum(f) / SR) + 0.3 * np.sin(4 * np.pi * np.cumsum(f) / SR)
    return stereo(norm(x * ex(t, 6) * np.minimum(1, t / 0.004), 0.4) * vol), 0
def s_ding(vol=0.5):
    return reverb(bell(1568, 1.0), 0.8, 0.25) * vol, 0
def s_shutter(vol=0.8):
    t = T(0.14); x = fft_filter(noise(len(t)), 1500, 5000) * ex(t, 220)
    d = int(0.035 * SR); y = np.zeros(len(t)); y[:len(x)] += x * 0.8
    y[d:] += (fft_filter(noise(len(t)), 200, 1800) * ex(t, 120))[:len(t) - d] * 0.8 + (np.sin(2 * np.pi * 170 * t) * ex(t, 60))[:len(t) - d] * 0.4
    return stereo(norm(y, 0.5) * vol), 0
def s_wipe(vol=0.75):
    d = 0.42; x = sweep_noise(d, 500, 5500, 0.9); n = len(x); t = np.arange(n) / SR
    crack = np.zeros(n); idx = rng.integers(0, n, 70); crack[idx] = rng.standard_normal(70); crack = fft_filter(crack, 1500, 8000) * 6
    e = np.sin(np.pi * np.minimum(t / d, 1)) ** 1.3
    y = (x * 0.9 + crack * 0.5) * e
    return stereo(norm(y, 0.55) * vol), int(d * .5 * SR)
def s_riser(dur=1.2, vol=0.7):
    x = sweep_noise(dur, 400, 7500, 0.8); n = len(x); t = np.arange(n) / SR; p = t / dur
    pulse = 0.6 + 0.4 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * (4 + 14 * p) * t) ** 2
    y = x * pulse * p ** 1.6 * np.minimum(1, (dur - t) / 0.01)
    return stereo(norm(y, 0.5) * vol), n
SFX = dict(slide=lambda c: s_slide(c.get('vol', .7)), slap=lambda c: s_slap(c.get('vol', .8)), stamp=lambda c: s_stamp(c.get('power', 1), c.get('vol', 1)),
           scribble=lambda c: s_scribble(c.get('dur', 12), c.get('vol', .5)), tape=lambda c: s_tape(c.get('vol', .5)), pop=lambda c: s_pop(c.get('vol', .6)),
           boing=lambda c: s_boing(c.get('vol', .5)), ding=lambda c: s_ding(c.get('vol', .5)), shutter=lambda c: s_shutter(c.get('vol', .8)),
           wipe=lambda c: s_wipe(c.get('vol', .75)), riser=lambda c: s_riser(c.get('dur', 1.2), c.get('vol', .7)))

# ------------------------------------------------------------------ BGM
bgm = np.zeros((N, 2)); lead = np.zeros((N, 2)); rhythm = np.zeros((N, 2)); harm = np.zeros((N, 2))
for ev in TL['music']:
    s = smp(ev['f']); i = ev['i']; v = ev.get('v', 1.0)
    if i == 'pizz': place(lead, pizz(ev['note'], v), s)
    elif i == 'kalimba': place(lead, kalimba(ev['note'], v), s)
    elif i == 'marimba': place(lead, marimba(ev['note'], v), s)
    elif i == 'bass': place(harm, bass(ev['note'], v), s)
    elif i == 'uke': place(harm, uke(ev['notes'], v, ev.get('down', True)), s)
    elif i == 'pad': place(harm, pad(ev['notes'], ev['dur'], ev['atk'], v), s)
    elif i == 'shaker': place(rhythm, shaker(v), s)
    elif i == 'brush': place(rhythm, brush(v), s)
    elif i == 'kick': place(rhythm, kickb(v), s)
    elif i == 'clap': place(rhythm, clap(v), s)
    elif i == 'wood': place(rhythm, wood(v), s)
# lead plucks get a short bright ping-pong delay (1 eighth = 0.3 s @100bpm)
def pingpong(x, d=0.3, fb=0.3, n=3):
    o = x.copy()
    for k in range(1, n + 1):
        sh = int(d * SR * k); g = fb ** k; o[sh:, k % 2] += x[:-sh, 0 if k % 2 else 1] * g
    return o
lead = reverb(pingpong(lead), 0.7, 0.18)[:N]
bgm = rhythm * 1.0 + harm * 1.15 + lead * 0.95
bgm *= 0.6
sfxbus = np.zeros((N, 2))
for c in TL['cues']:
    sig, peak = SFX[c['type']](c); place(sfxbus, sig, smp(c['frame']) - peak)
mix = bgm + sfxbus * 0.9
total = int(TL['total'] / FPS * SR); mix = mix[:total]
fo = int(1.2 * SR); mix[-fo:] *= (np.linspace(1, 0, fo) ** 1.5)[:, None]
mix = np.tanh(mix * 0.9) / np.tanh(0.9); mix = mix / max(np.abs(mix).max(), 1e-9) * 0.9
pcm = (mix * 32767).astype('<i2')
with wave.open(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 'public/audio_raw.wav', 'wb') as w:
    w.setnchannels(2); w.setsampwidth(2); w.setframerate(SR); w.writeframes(pcm.tobytes())
print('wrote', len(mix) / SR, 's peak', np.abs(mix).max())
```

## A: `tools/master.sh`

```bash
#!/bin/bash
# 2-pass loudnorm (-14 LUFS, TP -1.5) of the raw synth mix -> public/audio.wav (48k, 16-bit)
set -e
IN=public/audio_raw.wav
M=$(ffmpeg -hide_banner -i $IN -af loudnorm=I=-14:TP=-2.7:LRA=9:print_format=json -f null - 2>&1 | sed -n '/^{/,/^}/p')
g() { echo "$M" | python3 -c "import sys,json; print(json.load(sys.stdin)['$1'])"; }
ffmpeg -hide_banner -y -i $IN -af "loudnorm=I=-14:TP=-2.7:LRA=9:measured_I=$(g input_i):measured_TP=$(g input_tp):measured_LRA=$(g input_lra):measured_thresh=$(g input_thresh):offset=$(g target_offset):linear=true,aresample=48000" -c:a pcm_s16le public/audio.wav
```

## A: `src/timeline.ts`

```ts
// Reads the SAME timeline.json that tools/synth_audio.py uses to synthesise the soundtrack.
import tl from './timeline.json';

export const TL = tl;
export const FPS = tl.fps;
export const TOTAL = tl.total;
const map = new Map<string, any>(tl.cues.map((c: any) => [c.id, c]));
export const cue = (id: string): number => {
  const c = map.get(id);
  if (!c) throw new Error('missing cue ' + id);
  return c.frame;
};
export const SC = tl.scenes as unknown as Record<string, [number, number]>;
export const decay = (f: number, at: number, tau: number) => (f < at ? 0 : Math.exp(-(f - at) / tau));
const kicks: number[] = tl.kicks;
export const kickPulse = (f: number, tau = 5) => {
  let last = -1;
  for (let i = 0; i < kicks.length && kicks[i] <= f; i++) last = kicks[i];
  return last < 0 ? 0 : Math.exp(-(f - last) / tau);
};
/** thuds that shake / punch the paper camera */
export const IMPACTS: {frame: number; power: number}[] = tl.cues
  .filter((c: any) => c.type === 'stamp' || c.type === 'slap')
  .map((c: any) => ({frame: c.frame, power: c.type === 'stamp' ? c.power ?? 0.6 : 0.3}));
export const CUTS: number[] = tl.cuts;
export const PROJ: number[] = tl.projects;
export const inScene = (f: number, name: string) => f >= SC[name][0] && f < SC[name][1];
/** 0 before the piece starts moving, ~1 exactly on `at` (the cue / SFX frame) with a little paper settle after */
export const lp = (f: number, at: number, dur = 9, over = 0.07) => {
  const t = (f - (at - dur)) / dur;
  if (t <= 0) return 0;
  if (t < 1) return 1 - Math.pow(1 - t, 3);
  const k = f - at;
  return 1 + over * Math.sin(k * 0.9) * Math.exp(-k / 4);
};
export const clamp01 = (x: number) => Math.max(0, Math.min(1, x));
export const prog = (f: number, a: number, dur: number) => clamp01((f - a) / dur);
```

```ts
  cream: '#f3e9d2', cream2: '#ecdfc0', fiber: '#faf3e3', kraft: '#d6b985', mustard: '#e8ae2b', tomato: '#e2502d',
  teal: '#23807f', navy: '#1c2a4d', pink: '#f0a0b4', ink: '#1d1b1a', white: '#fbf6ea', brown: '#7a4e2d',
};

export const DISP = '"Abril Fatface", "DM Serif Display", serif';          // Latin-only display words
export const SANS = '"Rubik", "Noto Sans CJK SC", sans-serif';             // body / labels
```
