## B: `starter/src/Video.tsx`

```tsx
import React from 'react';
import {AbsoluteFill, Audio, staticFile, useCurrentFrame} from 'remotion';
import {Bg} from './Bg';
import {Camera, Overlay} from './Camera';
import {Grain, PaperDefs} from './paper';
import {PAL} from './theme';
import {Open} from './scenes/Open';
import {Main} from './scenes/Main';
import {inScene, TL, TOTAL} from './timeline';

const DEBUG = false; // true: prints frame number + cues firing within +-1 frame (handy when checking sync)

export const Video: React.FC = () => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame();
  const firing = DEBUG ? TL.cues.filter((c: any) => Math.abs(c.frame - f) <= 1).map((c: any) => c.id).join('  ') : '';
  return (
    <AbsoluteFill style={{background: PAL.cream}}>
      <Audio src={staticFile('audio.wav')} />
      <PaperDefs />
      <Camera>
        <Bg />
        {/* the whole scene layer goes through the global "rough" displacement filter (hand-cut wobble, re-seeded every 3 f) */}
        <AbsoluteFill style={{filter: 'url(#rough)'}}>
          {inScene(f, 'open') && <Open />}
          {inScene(f, 'main') && <Main />}
        </AbsoluteFill>
      </Camera>
      {/* end: fade to paper colour */}
      <AbsoluteFill style={{background: PAL.cream, opacity: Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (f - (TOTAL - 22)) / 20))}} />
      <Overlay />
      <Grain />
      {DEBUG && <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 20, bottom: 20, fontFamily: 'monospace', fontSize: 28, background: '#000a', color: '#fff', padding: 8}}>f{f} {firing}</div>}
    </AbsoluteFill>
  );
};
```

## B: `starter/src/scenes/Open.tsx`

```tsx
import React from 'react';
import {AbsoluteFill, useCurrentFrame} from 'remotion';
import {PAL, SERIF} from '../theme';
import {Layer, Piece, Shape, Stroke, circlePts, loopD, tw, useBoil} from '../paper';
import {Origami} from '../icons';
import {cue, lp} from '../timeline';

/** Cold open (bar 0): three phrase strips land ON beats 2,3,4 (cues open.s0/s1/s2); the third is stamped and circled. */
const STRIPS = [
  {t: 'PAPER', c: PAL.navy, tc: PAL.cream, rot: -3, at: 'open.s0', from: [-1100, 0], r: -10},
  {t: 'MEETS', c: PAL.tomato, tc: PAL.white, rot: 2, at: 'open.s1', from: [1300, 0], r: 10},
  {t: '代码', c: PAL.mustard, tc: PAL.ink, rot: -2, at: 'open.s2', from: [0, -60], r: 0},
];
const Strip: React.FC<{i: number}> = ({i}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame();
  const s = STRIPS[i];
  const size = 150, w = tw(s.t, size) * 1.15 + 90, h = 200;
  const stamp = i === 2;
  const p = lp(f, cue(s.at), stamp ? 6 : 10, 0.06);            // lands exactly on the cue frame
  const boil = useBoil('strip' + i, 1.2, 0.5);                  // stop-motion jitter every 3 frames
  const st = stamp ? 1 + (1 - Math.min(p, 1)) * 0.7 : 1;        // the stamp comes down from a larger scale
  return (
    <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 130, top: 190 + i * 215, width: w, height: h, opacity: p > 0.001 ? 1 : 0, transformOrigin: '0 0',
      transform: `translate(${s.from[0] * (1 - p)}px, ${s.from[1] * (1 - p)}px) scale(${st}) rotate(${s.rot + s.r * (1 - p)}deg) ${boil}`}}>
      <Piece x={0} y={0} w={w} h={h} color={s.c} seed={`strip${i}`} amp={3.4} fiber={6} pattern={i === 1 ? 'stripes' : i === 0 ? 'dots' : undefined} patColor="#000">
        <div style={{fontFamily: SERIF, fontWeight: 700, fontSize: size, color: s.tc, letterSpacing: 4, WebkitTextStroke: `3px ${s.tc}`, lineHeight: 1, whiteSpace: 'nowrap'}}>{s.t}</div>
      </Piece>
      {stamp && <Stroke d={loopD(w / 2, h / 2, w / 2 + 70, h / 2 + 45, 'cl', 1.12)} at={cue('open.circle')} dur={14} color={PAL.tomato} width={12} />}
    </div>
  );
};

export const Open: React.FC = () => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame();
  const folds = [0, 1, 2, 3].map((i) => lp(f, cue(`open.f${i}`), 12, 0.05));   // faces taped down on cues open.f0..f3
  return (
    <AbsoluteFill>
      <Layer depth={0.25}>
        <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 1000, top: 70, transform: `scale(${0.7 + 0.3 * Math.min(lp(f, 2, 14, 0.04), 1.05)})`, transformOrigin: '420px 420px'}}>
          <Shape pts={circlePts(420, 420, 420, 30)} color={PAL.navy} seed="sun" amp={9} fiber={9} pattern="dots" patColor="#fff" />
        </div>
        <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: -140, top: 640, transform: 'rotate(-8deg)'}}><Shape pts={[[0, 420], [330, 0], [700, 420]]} color={PAL.teal} seed="tri1" amp={8} fiber={8} pattern="stripes" patColor="#fff" /></div>
      </Layer>
      <Layer depth={1}>
        <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 1300 - 450 + 220, top: 520 - 450 + 60, width: 900, height: 900, transform: 'scale(0.8)', filter: 'drop-shadow(3px 9px 9px rgba(70,42,16,0.34))'}}>
          <Origami size={900} rotY={1.0 + f * 0.024} rotX={0.5} fold={folds} />
        </div>
        {STRIPS.map((_, i) => <Strip key={i} i={i} />)}
      </Layer>
    </AbsoluteFill>
  );
};
```

## B: `starter/src/scenes/Main.tsx`

```tsx
import React from 'react';
import {AbsoluteFill, useCurrentFrame} from 'remotion';
import {PAL, SERIF, DISP, SANS} from '../theme';
import {Boil, Land, Layer, Piece, Shape, Stamp, Stroke, Tape, arrowD, circlePts, rectPts, tw, useBoil} from '../paper';
import {Bulb, Laptop, Notebook, Star} from '../icons';
import {cue, kickPulse, lp} from '../timeline';

/** Scene 'main' (after the cut). Rule: first landing = cut + 9 f (the wipe still uncovers the scene), then beats. */
const WORD = 'starter';
const LW = [150, 150, 150, 150, 150, 150, 150];
const LC = [PAL.mustard, PAL.tomato, PAL.teal, PAL.navy, PAL.pink, PAL.mustard, PAL.tomato];
const LT = [PAL.ink, PAL.white, PAL.white, PAL.cream, PAL.ink, PAL.ink, PAL.white];

const Tile: React.FC<{i: number; at: number}> = ({i, at}) => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame();
  const p = lp(f, at + i * 3, 8, 0.1);                           // 3-frame stagger per letter, first one lands on the stamp cue
  const b = useBoil('t' + i, 1.5, 0.8);
  const x0 = 130 + i * 158;
  return (
    <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: x0, top: 120 + (i % 2) * 14, width: LW[i], height: 230, opacity: p > 0.001 ? 1 : 0,
      transform: `translateY(${-(1 - p) * 700}px) rotate(${[-4, 3, -2, 4, -3, 2, -3][i] + (1 - p) * (i % 2 ? 30 : -30)}deg) ${b}`}}>
      <Piece x={0} y={0} w={LW[i]} h={230} color={LC[i]} seed={`tile${i}`} amp={3.2} fiber={6} pattern={i % 3 === 0 ? 'dots' : undefined} patColor="#000">
        <div style={{fontFamily: DISP, fontSize: 210, color: LT[i], lineHeight: 1, marginTop: -22}}>{WORD[i]}</div>
      </Piece>
    </div>
  );
};

const CARDS = [
  {t: 'Idea', Icon: () => <Bulb size={112} />, c: PAL.mustard, rot: -3},
  {t: 'Build', Icon: () => <Laptop size={150} />, c: PAL.teal, rot: 2},
  {t: '视频', Icon: () => <Notebook size={100} />, c: PAL.tomato, rot: -2},
];

export const Main: React.FC = () => {
  const f = useCurrentFrame();
  const k = kickPulse(f, 6);                                      // 1 on every kick, decays: beat-pulse
  const title = cue('main.title');
  return (
    <AbsoluteFill>
      <Layer depth={0.28}>
        <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 1400, top: 300, transform: `rotate(6deg) scale(${lp(f, title + 6, 14, 0.04)})`, transformOrigin: '300px 300px'}}>
          <Shape pts={circlePts(300, 300, 300, 30)} color={PAL.pink} seed="ic1" amp={8} fiber={9} pattern="dots" patColor="#a02040" />
        </div>
        <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 1480, top: -60, transform: 'rotate(18deg)'}}><Shape pts={[[0, 360], [180, 0], [380, 360]]} color={PAL.teal} seed="ic2" amp={6} fiber={8} pattern="stripes" patColor="#fff" /></div>
      </Layer>
      <Layer depth={0.6}>
        <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 1700, top: 650, transform: `rotate(${f * 0.8}deg)`}}><Star size={90} color={PAL.tomato} /></div>
      </Layer>
      <Layer depth={1}>
        {WORD.split('').map((_, i) => <Tile key={i} i={i} at={title} />)}
        {CARDS.map((c, i) => {
          const at = cue(`main.c${i}`), x = 130 + i * 400;
          return (
            <Land key={i} at={at} dur={9} y={420} rot={c.rot * 3} style={{position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: 0}}>
              <Piece x={x} y={440} w={300} h={216} rot={c.rot} color={PAL.white} seed={`card${i}`} amp={2.6} fiber={5} inner={{flexDirection: 'column', justifyContent: 'flex-start', paddingTop: 12}}>
                <div style={{height: 112, display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center'}}><c.Icon /></div>
                <div style={{fontFamily: SERIF, fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 60, color: PAL.navy, lineHeight: 1.1}}>{c.t}</div>
              </Piece>
              <Tape x={x + 100} y={422} rot={-4} color={`${c.c}cc`} />
            </Land>
          );
        })}
        {[0, 1].map((i) => {
          const a = arrowD([130 + i * 400 + 316, 550], [130 + (i + 1) * 400 - 18, 550], 0.12, `ar${i}`);
          return (
            <React.Fragment key={i}>
              <Stroke d={a.shaft} at={cue(`main.a${i}`)} dur={9} color={PAL.tomato} width={9} />
              <Stroke d={a.head} at={cue(`main.a${i}`) + 7} dur={4} color={PAL.tomato} width={9} />
            </React.Fragment>
          );
        })}
        <Land at={cue('main.tag')} dur={9} x={-1000} rot={-4} style={{position: 'absolute', left: 0, top: 0}}>
          <Piece x={130} y={740} w={tw('Beat-synced. Pure Remotion.', 54) * 1.0 + 90} h={96} rot={-0.8} color={PAL.navy} seed="tag" amp={2.4} fiber={4}>
            <div style={{fontFamily: SERIF, fontWeight: 700, fontSize: 54, color: PAL.cream, whiteSpace: 'nowrap'}}>Beat-synced. Pure Remotion.</div>
          </Piece>
        </Land>
        <Stamp text="DONE" at={cue('main.final')} size={90} color={PAL.tomato} rot={-9} font={DISP} weight={400} x={1330} y={820} />
        <div style={{position: 'absolute', left: 130, top: 900, opacity: lp(f, cue('main.tag'), 9) > 0 ? 0.9 : 0, transform: `scale(${1 + 0.05 * k})`, transformOrigin: '0 50%', fontFamily: SANS, fontWeight: 600, fontSize: 28, letterSpacing: 6, color: PAL.brown}}>100 BPM · 30 FPS · ONE TIMELINE</div>
      </Layer>
    </AbsoluteFill>
  );
};
```
